New Rituals
Candle Lore Rituals
Bind Animal Familiar (Rating18/-10%):  5 hours. The practi-
tioner performs this ritual and binds one animal familiar to her-
self.  The effects are identical to the spell Bind Familiar, with
the exception that no Will Force is given up, and that the famil-
iar must be a creature of type animal.  A Candle Lore practition-
er may only have one animal familiar at a time.
Divining Rod (Rating10/+30%): 1 hour.  The practitoner per-
forms an hour long ritual on a piece of wood and when the ritual
is complete, this piece of wood will point to the nearest source
of water when held and asked to find water.  The stick retains
this power for one day.
Locate Animal (Rating 14/+10%):  1 hour.  The practitoner can
find the location of one specific known and named animal.
Sense Animal (Rating 15/+5%):  2 hours.  The practitioner can
sense all animals of one type in a named area.  For example, the
practitioner could choose to sense all wolves in the Black
Forest.
Ritual Lore Rituals
Bind Magical Animal Familiar (Rating 20/-25%):  1 hour. The
practitioner performs this ritual and binds one animal or magical
animal familiar to herself.  The effects are identical to the spell
Bind Familiar, with the exception that no Will Force is given up.
The familiar must be a creature of type animal or magical ani-
mal.  A Ritual Lore practitioner may only have one animal
familiar and one magical animal familiar at a time (allowing for
two familiars). 
Impervious Forest (Rating 22/-35%):  30 minutes.  The practi-
tioner performs this ritual in an area of forest no larger than 100
yards per practitioner Title.  All of the plants within this area
become immune to fire and cannot burn as long as they remain
in this area.
Locate Magical Animal (Rating15/+0%): 10 minutes.  The
practitoner can find the location of one specific known and
named animal or magical animal.
Sense Magical Animal (Rating 16/-5%):  20 minutes.  The prac-
titioner can sense all animals or magical animals of one type in a
named area.  For example, the practitioner can sense all dire
wolves in the Black Forest.
Empathy Magic Rituals
Bind Magical Animal Familiar (Rating 18/-15%):  30 minutes.
The practitioner performs this ritual and binds one magical ani-
mal familiar to herself.  The effects are identical to the spell
Bind Familiar, with the exception that no Will Force is given up.
The familiar must be a creature of type animal or magical ani-
mal.  An Empathy Magic practitioner may only have one animal
familiar and one magical animal familiar at a time (allowing for
two familiars). Object: Blood. 
Plant Bond (Rating 18/-15%):  30 minutes. The practitioner
performs a ritual and binds one magical plant to himself.  The
effects are identical to the spell Symbiosis, with the exception
that no Will force is given up, and that the plant must be a crea-
ture of type magical plant.  An Empathy Magic practitioner may
only have one plant bond at any time.  Object: Leaf.
Plant Projection (Rating 17/-10%):  20 minutes.  The practition-
er imagines a tree or plant of her choosing, and after a ritual is
performed, the practitioner can sense life from that plant as the
skill Life Sense.  If a face has previously been carved into the
tree, or one has been shaped into the tree with the skill Wood
Lore, then the practitioner can see, hear and smell from the face
on the tree as well.  Object: Leaf.
Tree Meld (Rating 22/-35%): 1 minute.  The practitoner per-
forms a brief ritual and then literally melds into a tree or plant
larger than the practitioners body.  The practitioner can stay in
this form for one hour, and all possessions stay with the being
when the meld occurs.  The being can sense life from within the
plant identical to the skill Life Sense.
Object: Bark or leaf.
Sympathy Magic Rituals
Feral Instinct(Rating18/-15%):  Causes one named being to feel
and behave with the tendencies of one chosen animal for a full
day.  For example, if monkey was chosen, the focus being
would crave bananas, try to pick insects from the hair of others,
and make occasional ooh-ooh noises.  Object: Hair.
Lycanthropy (Rating22/-35%): This ritual causes one named
being to be afflicted with the lycanthropy of the practitioners
choice.  Object: Blood.
New Runes
Warding (Rating 18/-10%)
Magical Animals:  When inscribed on a being, one magical ani-
mal of a specific type who come within 15´ of the rune must
make a Control Resistance or flee the area immediately.
Perceived.
Rune of Dissolution (Rating 22/-30%): All plants and non-magi-
cal animals within sight of the rune wither and die, and cannot
grow back as long as the rune is exposed.  Radiating.
aspects_rebuild.qxd  11/1/01  7:13 AM  Page 149

===== PAGE 158 =====
150
Evocation (Rating 20/-20%):  When a Rune of Evocation is
inscribed upon the body of an individual, the being gains one
animal ability as per the skill Evoke.  Each Evocation Rune
gives a separate Evoke ability, and a being may only have one
evocation rune inscribed upon his body at a time.  For example,
a Rune of Large Claws allows the being to evoke the ability
Large Claws at will (each ability takes 2-7 second to evoke).
Emanating.
New Songs
Animal Entrancement (Rating 15/+15%): All animals of one
named type who hear the song and fail a Control Resistance will
come to the singer and follow any requests he wishes for as long
as the song is sung.  If a request will cause harm to the animal,
the animal gets a new Control Resistance.  If the singer attempts
to entrance an animal who has been tamed by the skill Tame
Animal, then the Song Lore practitioner must win a contest of
skills with the Tame Animal practitioner to entrance the animal.
Tree Parting (Rating 17/+0%):  This song will cause all plants
and tree branches to bend and sway out of the path of the singer,
creating a walkway through areas of impassible vegetation.  The
plants will return to their normal position after the singer has
passed.  This song will take its effect on plants or plant walls
created from spells or invocations, but will not affect creatures
of type Magical Plant.
Tranquillity (Rating16/+5%):  A sense of peace  overcomes all
animals who hear the song, and they will cease all hostile action
and will initiate no new hostile actions while the song is being
sung.  If attacked, animals are allowed a new Control Resistance
to fight back.  Only animals which have been tamed with the
Tame Animal skill are allowed an initial Control Resistance; all
animals are allowed one if attacked.  Does not affect magical
animals.
New Poems
Water Finding (Rating14/+10%):  This poem will cause all ani-
mals who hear it to go to their nearest known natural source of
drinking water.  Does not affect magical animals.
Soothing (Rating 16/+0%):  All animals who hear the poem are
allowed a new Control Resistance to break free of any spell,
invocation, poem, song or ballad by which they are currently
controlled.  Does not affect magical animals.
New Ballads
Greater Soothing (Rating 20/-20%): All magical animals who
hear the ballad are allowed a new Control Resistance to break
free of any spell, invocation, poem, song or ballad by which
they are currently controlled.
Greater Animal Entrancement  (Rating 21/-20%): All magical
animals of one named type who hear the song and fail a Control
Resistance will come to the balladeer and follow any requests
the balladeer wishes for as long as the ballad is played.  If a
request will cause harm to the animal or magical animal, the
creature  gets a new Control Resistance.  If the Balladeer
attempts to entrance an animal who has been tamed by the skill
Tame Animal, then the Ballad Lore practitioner must win a con-
test of skills with the Tame Animal practitioner to entrance the
animal.
Greater Tranquillity (Rating 20/-15%):  A sense of peace  over-
comes all animals and magical animals who hear the song, and
they will cease all hostile action and will initiate no new hostile
actions while the song is being sung.  If attacked, animals and
magical animals are allowed a new Control Resistance to fight
back.
Plant Entrancement (Rating 20/-15%): All magical plants of
one named type who hear the song and fail a Control Resistance
will follow any requests the balladeer wishes for as long as the
ballad is played.  If the request will cause harm to the magical
plant, the magical plant gets a new Control Resistance.
New Potions
Animal Avoidance: (Optional Experience Value: 50)
Effects: Masks user's scent, muffles user's sounds and makes the
affected being invisible. These effects only relate to animals and
magical animal perceptions. Magical Animals can make a
Control Resistance to avoid the effects of this potion.
Duration: 3d4 hours
Regeneration: (Optional Experience Value: 300)
Effects: Grants Greater Regeneration, 1d4 points are healed per
10 seconds to all wounded areas (including vitals). Cannot
regenerate from fire or acid damage or if head and heart are sep-
arated. 
Duration: 1d4+2 hours
Potions Recipes 
Animal Avoidance: (lumpy brown potion)
 2 eagle, falcon or hawk eyes
 Nose of wolf or bloodhound
 16 rodent feet 
 1 pint of magical spring water
 Bring water to a boil over a magical fire.  Pulp eyes and add.
Grind rodent feet and add. Grind nose and add. Stir until mix-
ture boils. Reduce heat, simmer until materials congeal. Let
cool.  Bottle by starlight.

===== PAGE 159 =====
151
Potion of Regeneration: (syrupy red/brown potion)
 1 pint holy water or water from a magical source
 1 acorn, finely ground
 5 drops of blood from a severed Hydra head
 1 yolk from a Hydra egg
 Bring holy water to a boil over a magical fire.  Add acorn.
Drizzle the blood into the mixture.  Add yolk.  Boil for 3 min-
utes.  Bottle while warm.
Potion of Enhanced Hearing: (very viscous red/black potion)
 1 pint Manticore blood or 2 finely ground ears and 1 pint of
dog's blood
 10 drops of water from a cold mountain spring
 Bring blood to a boil over a normal fire.  Add water.  Boil for
an additional 2 minutes.  Cool.  Allow to stand for one hour.
Skim.  Bottle.
Potion of Enhanced Speed: (red diluted potion)
 1 pint of water from a lowland stream
 1 drop of Cockatrice blood or adrenal gland of a cheetah 
 1 ounce of ash from a summer brush fire
 Add blood to water and bring to a boil over a normal fire.
After boiling for 2 minutes, stir in the ashes.  Boil for an addi-
tional minute.  Allow to cool.  Bottle.
Potion of Endure Heat: (very viscous sticky yellow/red potion)
 1/2 pint of Fire Drake blood or Red Basilisk blood or Fire
Hydra blood
 10 grams of ground Fire Drake scales or Red Basilisk scales or
Fire Hydra scales
 Mix the blood together, stirring vigorously, sprinkle in scales.
Place in a vial.  Immerse in magically chilled water for 1 day.
Bottle while cold.
Potion of Endure Acid: (very viscous yellow/green potion)
 1/2 pint of Spitting Drake blood or Spitting Hydra blood
 10 grams of ground Spitting Drake scales or Spitting Hydra
scales
 Mix the blood together, stirring vigorously, sprinkle in scales.
Place in a vial.  Immerse in vinegar for 1 day.  Bottle.
Potion of Flying: (viscous black potion)
 1 pint of water from a salt lake
 7 drops of Wyvern blood or Griffon blood or Pegasus blood
 1 Wyvern scale or 1 Griffon feather or 1 Pegasus feather
 Combine blood and water.  Bring to a boil over a pine wood
fire.  Add feather.  Boil for 3 minutes.   Remove and discard
feather.  Bottle mixture when cooled.
Potion of Curing: (translucent golden yellow potion)
 1/2 pint of holy water
 3 ounces of pulverized gargoyle (slain within the past 24
hours)
 1/3 of a dose of a potion of healing or 6 oz. of ground Unicorn
horn
 Have all ingredients blessed by a good priest.  Add the healing
potion (or ground horn) to the holy water.  Bring to a boil over a
sacred flame.  Stir in the powdered gargoyle with a blessed crys-
tal rod.  Boil for 3 minutes.  Strain.  Bottle when cooled.
Anti-toxin: (translucent shades of green or brown potions)
 1/2 a pint of holy water or water from a magical source.
 Ground/pulped venomous animal/plant part (determines Anti-
toxin level, see below)
 6 drops of blood from any magical humanoid
 Use a wooden mixing bowl, add water first. Sprinkle in
pulped/ground animal/plant part. Add 6 drops of blood one at a
time. Seal, bury in blessed or magical earth. After 1 day recover
and bottle.
Note: Anti-Toxin level is always at the same level of the venom
the animal or plant produced. Thus a type V poison stinger pro-
duces a type V Anti-toxin potion regardless of potency.
Invisibility: (translucent barely visible gray potion)
 1/2 pint blood of a phase tiger or sphixian cat
 3 whiskers of a phase tiger or 6 of a sphixian cat
 yolk of a cockatrice egg or basilisk eye fluid
 Combine blood and yolk (or fluid).  Bring to a boil over a
magical fire.  Heat until boiling, add a whisker, simmer for 10
minutes and then repeat process until no whiskers remain. Bottle
mixture while hot and place in darkened container for 6 hours.
Wondrous Warrior: (iridescent blue potion)
 1 pint blood of manticore or hydra (any type)
 3 ground manticore quills or 3 ground griffon claws
 yolk of a rocs egg or whole griffon egg
 2 ground berserker roots.
 Combine blood and yolk (or egg).  Simmer over a blessed and
magical fire, add in ground berserker roots.  Heat until boiling,
add in quills or claws a pinch at a time over the course of an
hour. Reduce heat and place in a steel container in the crook of a
black oak tree for 6 hours. Bottle.
Mystical Mage: (iridescent red potion)
 1/2 pint blood of giant spider
 1/2 pint of giant bee royal jelly
 1 ground silver thistle plant
 2 basilisks eyes or cockatrice eyes
 Combine blood and melt in jelly.  Simmer over a blessed and
magical fire, add in silver thistle plant.  
 Heat until boiling, add eyes. Boil until eyes disappear. Cover.
Place in a strong aura residue field for 6 hours. Bottle.

MAGICAL ITEMS
ARMBAND OF THE BEAR
Description:  This armband is made of the highest quality silver, engraved with
the ornate image of a bear.  Once per day, it allows the wearer to increase his
Strength by 3 points, thus raising all skills and effects based upon Strength (such
as weapon damage and melee to hit bonus). The items power takes 5 seconds to
activate by thought, and the increased Strength effect will last 3 hours.  The arm-
band provides its own Aura and does not requires the wearer to have any avail-
able Aura in his own pool.
Base Aura Level 18
Powers 
Enhance Physical (NR):  1 x day, by thought
Total PP Spent: 34 
Classification: Minor      Time to Enchant: 12 Hours
Will Force Cost: 1/10     Exp. Value: 1,300           Gp. Cost: 600                       
Design Notes: The armband could be replaced by a similar piece of jewelry of
equal value.  
ARMBAND OF THE BULL
Description:  This armband is made of the highest quality blue steel and bears
the relief image of a charging bull.  Once per day, it allows the wearer to
increase her Vitality by 3 points, thus raising all skills and effects based upon
Vitality (such as End and Poison Resistance). The items power takes 5 seconds
to activate by thought, and the increased Vitality effect will last 3 hours.  The
armband provides its own Aura and does not requires the wearer to have any
available Aura in her own pool.
Base Aura Level 18
Powers 
Enhance Physical (NR):  1 x day, by thought
Total PP Spent: 34 
Classification: Minor       Time to Enchant: 12 Hours
Will Force Cost: 1/10    Exp. Value: 1,300            Gp. Cost: 500    
Design Notes: The armband could be replaced by a similar piece of jewelry of
equal value.
ARMBAND OF THE LION
Description:  This armband is made of the highest quality gold, and bears the
engraved image of an attacking lion.  Once per day, it allows the wearer to
increase his Strength, Agility and Vitality by 2 points each, thus raising all skills
based upon physical attributes, as well as effects based on each  individual phys-
ical attribute. The items power takes 5 seconds to activate by thought, and the
increased physical attribute effect will last 3 hours.  The armband provides its
own Aura and does not requires the wearer to have any available Aura in his
own pool.
Base Aura Level 18

equal value.

the etched image of the stripe pattern of a tiger.  Once per day, it allows the
wearer to increase his Agility by 3 points, thus raising all skills and effects based
upon Agility (such as weapon damage and melee to hit bonus). The items

BOW OF MISSILES
Description: The Bow of Missiles is a finely crafted wooden long bow which has
the ability to keep the wielder supplied with an endless amount of magical arrows.
When the words, an arrow in need are spoken, a magical wooden arrow with a
metal tip is created and placed ready to fire in the bow.  These arrows give +3 to
hit and +3 to damage.  Creating the arrow takes 1 second, and saying the phrase
takes 1 second, making the arrows load in 2 seconds.  The bow still takes its nor-
mal time to fire (3 seconds, 2 seconds minimum) in addition to this 2 second load-
ing time.
The bow has an additional power as well.  When the words arrows of light
seek the fight are spoken by the wielder, the bow will begin firing a series of
eight arrows in 2 second intervals at any target(s) for which the user aims. These
arrows do 1d6+1 damage each and will pass through normal armor.  Magical
armor will stop arrows on a successful Magic Resistance roll, made for each indi-
vidual arrow.  When firing these arrows, the wielder of the bow must still roll to
hit as if firing the bow normally (however these arrows are not +3 to hit).  This
power of the bow takes 5 seconds to activate, can be used three times per day, and
requires that the wielder have at least 11 free Aura points in his Aura Pool.
Base Aura Level 16
Abilities
Produce Wood: Constant, by word (used to create the arrows)
Produce Metal:  Constant, by word (used to create the arrows)
Powers
Storm of Arrows (Aura Level 11):  3 x day, by word
Total PP Spent: 213    Classification: Major       Time to Enchant: 4 months
Will Force Cost: 1        Exp. Value: 1,400             Gp. Cost: 25,000                    
Designer Notes: The bow could also be crafted as any other type of bow except
crossbow.
BOW OF SEEKING
Description:  This bow is of the highest quality elven craftmanship, created from
the wood of a fallen ancient oak.  When the words fly true are spoken, the bow
will enchant any arrow fired from it with the power of accuracy, thus giving the
arrow +3 to hit and +3d6+3 damage.  On a natural roll of 18+, the total damage
from the arrow will double.  
The bow has a second ability.  Whenever any loaded arrow is stroked across
the bow for 2 seconds before being fired, the arrow will gain an additional +1 to
hit and +1 to damage and gain the power of the spell Seeking.  The wielder must
roll to hit as normal, but the arrow will seek out any target by moving around
obstacles, corners or obstructions, so long as the wielder correctly imagined the
target (by making a successful Intelligence save).  If the target is hidden, the
arrow will circle the area for 15 seconds waiting for an opportunity to strike, at
which point it will finally fall to the ground if it has not found its target. The
range of the bow is increased three times when fired this way, and the arrow will
fall to the ground if it leaves this range. 
Base Aura Level 15
Abilities
Accuracy: Constant, by word (placing the arrow in the bow, 1 second)
Seeking:  Constant, by deed (stroking the arrow against the bow, 2 seconds)
Total PP Spent: 280     Classification: Major      Time to Enchant: 4 months
Will Force Cost: 1         Exp. Value: 2,500            Gp. Cost: 30,000                     
Designer Notes: The bow could also be crafted as any other type of bow except
crossbow.

CLAWS OF THE SPIDER
Description: These claws are made from the highest quality steel, and are 
identical to the weapon cats claws in the Players Guide.  The claws have two
powers, both which take 5 seconds to activate.  The first power is that of the
spell Climb, and will enable the wielder to adhere to any surface and climb
along it like a spider.  The wielder may carry only half his load limit.  This
power can be used any number of times per day, but each use only lasts up to 20
minutes and must then be reactivated.  The user must have at least 2 free Aura in
his pool to activate this power.
The second power is that of the spell Adhere, and will cause any one object to
stick to the claws.  If this is a being, the individual gets a Magic Resistance, but
if failed the being is stuck and can only be freed if he either makes a Force roll
or can exert over 200 pounds with his load limit.  This second power can be
used any number of times per day, but the effect will last for only 10 minutes
and must then be reactivated.  The user must have at least 3 free Aura in his pool
to activate this power.
Base Aura Level 20
Powers
Climb (Aura Level 2):  At will, by thought
Adhere (Aura Level 3):  At will, by thought
Total PP Spent: 75 
Classification: Minor         Time to Enchant: 1 week
WF Cost: 1/10th
Exp. Value: 400                Gp. Cost: 500
CLAWS OF THE WOLVERINE
Description: These claws, made from the highest quality steel, are identical to
the weapon tigers claws in the Players Guide.  When used to strike with a
thrusting or cutting attack, the claws will gain +3 to hit and do 5d6+3 damage,
and the total damage is doubled on natural roll of 18+.  The claws are activated
by the wielder saying the words wolverine claws, an action which takes a total
of 3 seconds.  The claws ability can be turned on and off an unlimited number
of times.  No Aura is needed from the wielder.  
Base Aura Level 15
Abilities
Piercing: Constant, by word (by extending claws; +1 additional seconds). 
Sharpness:  Constant, by word (by extending claws; +1 additional seconds). 
Total PP Spent: 300 
Classification: Major       Time to Enchant: 6 months
Will Force Cost: 2
Exp. Value: 800              Gp. Cost: 30,000                  
CLOAK OF THE WOLVERINE
Description: This shimmering cloak, made from a fine thin leather, has one
ability and two powers of speed and stealth.  The cloak has the ability to blink
its wearer, an ability that takes one second and causes its wearer to be teleported
to any location within sight.  After this teleportation is complete, the wearer
must spend one second and make an Intelligence save or be disoriented, and if
failed can do nothing for 1d4+1 seconds.  The wearer can use this ability in the
next combat round if a successful Will Force save is made.  
In addition, three times per day, the wearer can spend 5 seconds and activate
the power of distortion.  This power makes the wearer appear to twist and dis-
tort, and all attacks against the wearer have an automatic 50% chance of miss-
ing.  This power will last for 3 minutes when activated, and the user must have
at least 7 free Aura to activate this power.  
The last power of the item will cover the user in a minor visual illusion that
causes him to appear to be part of the surface which he is standing against.  This
is illusionary in nature, and can be avoided with a successful Illusion Resistance.
This last power takes 5 seconds to activate, can be used three times per day, and
will last up to 18 hours when activated. The user must have 2 free Aura to acti-
vate this power.
Base Aura Level 18
Abilities
Blink:  constant, by thought 
Powers
Distortion (7):  at will, by thought (3x per day)
Hide (2):  at will, by thought (3x per day)
Total PP Spent: 376
Classification: Arch       Time to Enchant: 10 months
WF Cost: 1/2
Exp. Value: 400             Gp. Cost: 80,000                   
Designer Notes: The name of this cloak can be changed to fit any animal of
speed or stealth.
CLOAK OF THE WILD
Description: This finely sewn cloak seems to shimmer and change with the
background behind it.  If an individual wearing the cloak stands still and imag-
ines for 5 second that he is disappearing, the cloak will take on the color of the
surrounding landscape, and the wearer will gain +20% to the Move Unseen and
Blend skills.  This effect will last for 2 hours and must then be reactivated; also
if the wearer moves into a different color terrain type the cloak must be reacti-
vated to make it match the new environment.
The wearer of the cloak may also activate a second power, that of the spell
Night Vision.  A third power, that of the spell Enhanced Smell, can be activated
and will give the wearer +36% to the Smell skill. (If the wearer does not have
the Smell skill, he gets it at base chance +36% while the power is active.)  These
last two powers both take 5 seconds to activate and will last for 12 hours each
before they must be reactivated. 
Because the cloak is made of a fine magical cloth, it also affords its wearer an
additional -2 defensive adjustment from all attacks against him.  None of the
powers of the item require the user to have any free Aura in his Aura Pool.
Base Aura Level 12
Powers
Color (NR):  at will, by thought
Night Vision (NR):  at will, by thought
Enhanced Smell (NR):  at will, by thought
Total PP Spent: 210
Classification: Major      Time to Enchant: 3 months
WF Cost: 1
Exp. Value: 400              Gp. Cost: 8000                     
Designer Notes: Sometimes these cloaks are created with an affinity only for
one race (usually elves).  This drops the total power points used to 158, the time
to enchant 3 months and the value to 6000 gold.
DEEP TENT OF FOLDING
Description: This tent is a miniature folded tent, a mere one square inch in size.
When thrown to the ground, the tent will expand into a full-sized tent of strong
magical fabric, now 8´ wide x 8´ long x 4´ high, in the shape of a pyramid.
Opening the door to the tent causes a room to appear, which is a separate dimen-
sional space, exactly as the spell Dimension.  Inside the space is a pyramid 80´
wide x 80´ long x 40´ high, but from the outside the tent appears as normal.
When the user pats the tent on its outside and thinks of it shrinking, the tent will
fold back to one square inch in size.  If the tent is closed while anyone or any-
thing is inside, the tent will be destroyed as it contracts around the objects or
beings.  Anyone inside will take 4d6 damage to 3d6 random areas, and any
objects inside will be damaged (metal objects lose 2d4 from their object
strength, and wooden and other objects lose 4d6 from their object strength.)  No
Aura is required to use the tent, and its effects can be used an unlimited number
of times.
Base Aura Level 20
Abilities
Dimension: constant, by deed (when the door to the tent is opened, 3 seconds).  
Size Change:  constant, by deed (when tent is thrown to the ground, 3 seconds)
Total PP Spent: 300
Classification: Major       Time to Enchant: 6 months
Will Force Cost: 2       Exp. Value: 3,500              Gp. Cost: 6d10 x 1000

FAIRY NET
Description: The Fairy Net is made from the highest quality magical silk spun
from a giant spider, giving it a bonus of +4 to hit and +4 to damage in combat.
The user must roll to hit, and the following effects apply:
1. Upon a successful hit, the target loses 1d4 -2 seconds and must make an
Agility save or be entangled in the net.  As the net is not hooked, it does no base
damage, thus it always does 4 magic bonus) when used to attack.
2. If any part of the net hits a Fairy, in addition to the above, the Fairy must
make a make a Magic Resistance or be stuck to the net as per the spell Adhere.
The adhesion lasts until the owner wills the power of the net to deactivate and
thus release the victims.  The item requires no Aura from the user.
Base Aura Level 20
Abilities
Adhere:  constant, by thought; attuned against fairies.
Total PP Spent: 15
Classification: Minor       Time to Enchant: 6 Hours
Will Force Cost: 1/10     Exp. Value: 400               Gp. Cost: 50                       
Designer Notes: Similar nets could be designed which were attuned against
other races, but they would be not nearly as effective, due to the low Strength
rating of most fairies (anyone with a load limit of over 200 can break the adhe-
sion, as can a successful Force roll).
FLUTE BLOWGUN OF POISONING

flute is blown with all holes of the flute covered for 4 seconds, a high note will
play and a +4 metal dart will appear in the flute blowgun, coated with an instan-
taneous poison of type XX, potency Q.  If the dart does flesh damage to a crea-
ture (must enter the bloodstream), the creature will die instantly if it fails its
Poison Resistance.  If a Poison Resistance is made, it will suffer 1d4 points of
damage per minute to its base End until the poison leaves its system (the dura-
tion of the poison is 20 days, or until removed through magic).  The magic dart
is +3 to hit and +3 to damage.  The item requires no Aura from the user.

destroyed.  No Aura is required by the user to operate this item.

SEED OF THE MIGHTY OAK
Description: The item appears as a near perfect oak seed.  When this seed is
planted, it will produce a 90 year old oak tree that continues to grow as normal
thereafter.  When the tree first grows, the user should say the word drink and
the seed will provide its own water.  Thereafter, once a week, the user can pro-
vide the tree with its own water by saying the word drink.  The tree becomes
mildly sapient and can communicate thoughts and impressions to the planter of
the seed.  Also, five times per day, the planter can say the words entwine and
6 seconds later the tree will begin entwining a victim as the spell Entwine.  This
means that any of the plants limbs can reach out up to 54 feet away and with a
successful roll to hit of 10+ can grab any being in reach.  This attack takes a full
ten seconds and has a 60% chance to grab the victim after the roll to hit has been
made.  Each 10 seconds thereafter, the grabbed victim may try and escape, but
the plant will keep its hold at a chance of 80%-2% per Strength rating of the vic-
tim.  This power will last 3 minutes and requires no Aura from the user. 
Base Aura Level 18

SEED OF THE MYSTIC OAK
Description: The item appears as a perfect oak seed.  When this is seed is
planted, it will produce an intelligent, sapient and speaking 90 year old oak tree
that continues to grow as normal thereafter.  When the tree first grows, the user
should say the words water me and the seed will provide its own water.
Thereafter, once a week, the tree can provide its own water by saying the words
water me.   Also, five times per day, the tree can say the word entangle and
6 seconds later its limbs will begin entwining a victim as the spell Entwine.
This means that any of the plants limbs can reach out up to 54 feet away and
with a successful roll to hit of 10+ can grab any being in reach.  This attack
takes a full ten seconds and has a 60% chance to grab the victim after the roll to
hit has been made.  Each 10 seconds thereafter, the grabbed victim may try and
escape, but the plant will keep its hold at a chance of 80%-2% per Strength rat-
ing of the victim;  this power will last 3 minutes.  The plant has a final ability:
three times per day it can become mobile for 3 minutes, gaining a movement of
3/30/3.  When 3 minutes is up the tree must replant itself.  The tree sees the
being which planted it as its creator, and will remain loyal and obedient to it, as
long as the being does not betray the trust of the tree.
Base Aura Level 18
Abilities
Plant Speak:  constant, by deed (activates when Plant Growth is activated) 
Plant Growth:  constant, by deed (activates when the seed is planted)

Designer Notes: The spell Endow Intelligence was cast on the seed before the

WOLFS LAIR RING
Description: The Wolf's Lair Ring is made of brass with a silver bas relief
image carved in a circular centerpiece.  The relief image is that of a wolf under a
tree gazing up at the stars. Surrounding the image is a leaf motif that trails off
down the sides of the ring. When the command words night wolf are whis-
pered, the wielder of the ring, along with all beings touching the ring (and their
items) are transported to an extra-dimensional lair (exactly as the spell Cache).
The ring does not go into the cache dimension, but instead remains behind and
blends into the surface where it was left (as the spell Hide). A powerful druid
created the cache as a secret hide-away; later this druid passed into the afterlife,
leaving the ring behind as a great lost treasure. The dimensional space appears
as in the map below. 
Base Aura Level N/A
Total PP Spent: 0         Classification: Minor         Gp. Cost: 50,000   
Design Notes: The druid first cast the spell Cache to create the extra-dimen-
sional space where she could hide away as needed. The ring was made as the
key to the Cache. Within the Cache she used the spell Dimension to increase the
internal space (as listed in the "notes" of the spell). She then used the spell Hide
on the ring so that whenever it was not worn, it would blend into the natural sur-
roundings. She moved in earth, grass and two young trees. She used the spells
Produce Water and Produce Light to give her plants the necessary sustenance,
and with Control Growth molded the grass and the trees to maturity.  All spells
were made permanent with the spell Permanency.      
The Wolfs Lair (map key)
Except where noted, all walls, floors and  ceilings are made of a gray granite
that is cool to the touch.  All ceilings are 10 feet high.  Doors are made of hard-
ened oak whose density has been increased with Wood Lore.  Each door has a
wolfs head handle with a long tongue, and the tongue  can be turned to open the
doors. All of the ceilings give off a diffuse magical light.
1. The Mouth
Any who enter the cache will be in the area marked with the encircled S. The
six pillars appear almost to be fangs, as each has been shaped into two long
cones whose points are joined in the center where they touch. The fangs are
rough in texture and have dimples all over their surface.  The door has no lock,
nor does the door at the end of the passage.
a. A pressure plate here deactivates the traps at b.  While it is being 
stood upon, the traps at b will not activate. 
b. A magical trap here is activated when anyone passes within 6 feet of 
a fang (pillar), shooting 36 magical darts (6 per pillar).  Each 
person within 12 feet of a pillar will be struck by 1d6 darts 
unless blocked by another person or object.  Each dart does 
2d4+2 and is coated in a type V, potency H magical paralysis 
poison.  The trap will magically reset after 15 minutes.
2. Right Fore Paw 
A small room with a well made bed, unlocked trunk and a tapestry of a gray
wolf running under a starry sky.
3. Left Fore Paw
A small room with a well-made bed, unlocked trunk and a tapestry of a brown
wolf and a gray wolf nuzzling each other.
4. Right Hind Paw
A long double bookcase holds out-of-date history books and has space for many
more volumes.

5. Left Hind Paw
This area appears to have been used as a storage area; several empty crates and
casks are piled about the room.
6.  The Tail
A once well used archery range. A bale of hay with a painted target is at the end
of the tail. A high quality long bow, composite bow and quiver with 60 arrows
hang from a rack to the right of the entrance.
7. Belly
This room is large and the floor in the center of the room is filled with earth and
grass. There is a stillpond in the center that has extremely clean and tasty water.
A young elm sapling crowds the ceiling in the north portion of the room and a
similar oak fills the south. The pond is 10 feet deep in the center and 3 feet deep
at the edges.
8. Heart
This secret room is accessed by swimming into the center of the pool at 7 and
down into the tunnel c.  It has a large table which has dusty outlines of a treasure
long removed. Tunnel c is 8 feet in circumference and 60 feet deep.  At the bot-
tom, the tunnel turns and makes a U, bending back upwards.  At the end of the
U a large wolf with an open mouth is in bas relief.  A handle can be felt when
placing ones hand inside the mouth.  Grasping the handle and pulling it results
in a round secret door opening into 8 (one must climb upwards out of the water
into the room).  Any other motion (left/right/push) results in 6d6 of crushing
damage per 5 seconds to the hand.  A successful Force skill roll can be used to
remove a hand from the wolfs bite.  One attempt per 5 seconds is allowed.
DIVINE MAGIC ITEMS
BLANKET OF COMFORT
Description: This fine quality wool blanket is interwoven with magical thread,
and it has the ability to keep its wearer at the exact perfect temperature for the
environment.  If the environment is cold, the wearer can spend one second and
activate the warming power of the blanket, and the blanket will heat up to 100
degrees higher than the surrounding temperature.  If the environment is hot, the
wearer can spend 1 second to activate the cooling power of the blanket, and the
blanket will cool down up to 100 degrees below the surrounding temperature.
Additionally, the wearer can spend 5 seconds in the blanket and cause it to heal
one wounded body area for 10d4+10 End.  This ability can be used twice per
day.

CHEST OF BEES
Description: This box appears as a finely carved golden box with strange carv-
ings of bees and honeycombs on its outside.  Once per day, the possessor can
spend 7 seconds, open the box and activate its power.  When activated, a swarm
of bees will emerge (under the control of the possessor), which can fly at 15 feet
per second and do 1d4 points of damage to all non-protected areas (armor slits
and eye holes count as unprotected, as does any armor of rating 2 or less.  Each
10 seconds of exposure to the swarm causes the victims to make a Poison
Resistance or they will suffer 10d4 damage to the neck and 10d8 damage to the
upper torso from respiratory inflammation.  After 3 minutes and 20 seconds, the
insects will vanish back into the box, and it will shut.  The possessor must have
at least an 18 Piety rating to operate this item.

FIGURINE OF THE HAWK
Description: This crystalline figurine has been crafted into the shape of a hawk
with its wings outspread.  When activated, the figurine will allow a tame animal
to deliver a message.  The animal will be granted limited sapience, and will fol-
low the instructions of the bearer of the item for up to 20 days.  The animal gets
a magical voice and gets an instinctive knowledge of where the recipient of the
message is.  The animal will accept a reply from the recipient of the message,
but it is still subject to the duration of 20 days.  If the animal messenger is ever
threatened by death, it must make a Control Resistance or it will be freed from
service and the message will not be delivered.  This item cannot be activated by
those with less than a Piety rating of 7.

FIGURINE OF THE PEGASUS
Description: The figurine is made of high quality mithreel, and is fashioned in
the shape of a pegasus.  Three times per day, when the bearer spends 5 seconds,
the figurine activates and a large semi-transparent magical beast in the same
shape as the figurine appears.  The beast can carry up to a ton in weight (2000
lbs.), has constant Life Sense at 100%, and can detect physical objects by touch.
The creature will accept only simple commands from whomever possesses the
figurine, and has a movement of 20 miles per hour (sustained) and 30 per second
in combat on the ground.   The beast has wings as well, and can fly at 90 feet
per second with a sustained speed of 60 miles per hour.  The beast is immune to
damage but can be dispelled or banished; in any case, the beast will disappear
after 10 hours of being activated, and another 5 seconds must be spent before the
beast can be reactivated.  In addition to the pegasus, the bearer can opt to sum-
mon a chariot  along with the beast.  This chariot can carry 10 riders.   The item
can only be activated by someone with a 9 Piety rating or more.
Base Piety Level 20

SEEDPOST OF FARMING

to imbue a 1000 feet diameter area surrounding it with positive life energy.
While within the area:
1) Waking beings are healed (at their healing rate) as if one day had passed.
Sleeping beings are healed as if one week had passed.  (Magical wounds
are also affected by the healing rate.)
2) Disease and poison effects and duration are halved.
3) A Control Resistance must be made to initiate combat, and only one such roll
can be made (those being attacked do not need to make a Control
Resistance to defend).

STAFF OF THE THICKET
Description: This gnarled and twisted staff is made from a very fine quality
polished wood.  The staff has the following powers:
1. Three times per day, when the staff is pointed at a plant no more than 40 feet
away, the plant will come alive and can entangle its victims.  This effect will last
for 3 minutes and 20 seconds, and the effect depends on the plant type:
Plant Type
Entangle
Chance of Holding Victim
Bush, thick
25%
50% - 2% per Strength rating of target
Tree, small
50%
60% - 2% per Strength rating of target

80%
90% - 2% per Strength rating of target
*Vines do an additional 3d6 constriction damage per 10 seconds in areas held.
Plant Type: The general type of plant being affected by Entwine.
Entangle: The chance per 10 seconds of entangling an actively moving target.
Non-moving targets are automatically entangled.
Holding: The chance occurring every 10 seconds of holding on to an entangled
target.
2.   Three times per day, when the staff is turned in the ground for 7 seconds, the
staff can help its wielder find any target on the same plane.  Only a vague sense
of distance to the target is known.  The GM may determine that either the goal is
unattainable or protected in some way so that the effect will fizzle.  This effect
lasts 20 minutes once activated.
3. Three times per day, when pointed at a plant no more than 60 feet away and
circular motions are made for 7 seconds, the plant (including a non-germinating
seed) will gain up to 60 years of growth.  Unwilling plants get a Magic
Resistance.
4. Three times per day, when the staff is tapped on the ground for 7 seconds, a
wall of thorns will appear, no more than 60 feet away.  The wall can be com-
posed of up to 20 sections, each 10 feet high by 10 feet wide and 3 feet thick.
The walls can be placed anywhere on a solid surface and may not be created on
top of beings.  Each section has an object threshold of 10 and an object strength
of 60.  Anyone attacking the wall will take 1d6 points of damage to all exposed
areas touching its surface.  The wall will last 100 minutes.
4. Three times per day, when the staff is shaken at a wooden target no more
than 40 feet away for 7 seconds, the wood will twist and warp unnaturally,
removing 20 points of object strength from the wood each time it is used.  This
effect will not work on magical wood.
Base Piety Level 20
Powers

Herbs
The Herb Lore skill applies to identification and usage of all herbs provided. If
the Herb is also poisonous then Poison Lore can be used to identify the herb, its
poison type and usage. Herbs that are magical in nature give off a residue of
Aura to the skills Detect Magic and Detect Aura and other similar effects or
powers.
Aloe Vera
Type: Leaf
Climate: Wet and Dry Tropical, Desert, Semi-arid (plains, brush, scrubland,
desert)
Magical: No
Value: 2-4 silver
Potency: 1 week, 1 year if sealed
Duration: Immediate 
Preparation/Usage: Pulped, applied to surface wounds/burns.
Powers: Heals 1d4+1 points to burns (including sunburn) or surface wounds
(including swelling causes by insect bites). 
Description: This plant has been known as lily of the desert, and the medi-
cine plant.  It is a plant with thick, tuberous, radial leaves green or red in color,
sometimes with small white spots. Each leaf provides 1-3 doses. The plant can
produce 1-2 new leaves per year in normal conditions. Also 7-8 doses can be
used at once to mask a humanoids odor.
Arnica
Type: Flower
Climate: Wet and Dry Tropical, Humid Coastal (sandy soil)
Magical: No
Value: 1-4 silver
Potency: 1 month, x12 if dried
Duration: Immediate 
Preparation/Usage: dried, ground, and added to oil/cream base, rubbed on sur-
face wounds or bruises.
Powers: Heals 1d4+1 points to cuts and bruises.  
Description: This plant is often referred to as leopards bane.  This daisy-like
flower blooms in midsummer. Each flowering plant can provide 3d4 doses.
Badger Thorn
Type: Thorn
Climate: Humid Continental, Semi-arid (grasslands, forest, hills)
Magical: No 
Value: 4-6 gold
Potency: 6 weeks, x2 if dried
Duration: 2-5 (1d4+1) hours
Preparation/Usage: Ground to powder and ingested
Powers: Endurance is raised by 50% or 1/2 again its current value for the dura-
tion.
Description: A bright red thorn, found on a leafy bush that grows in the lee of
hills or short mountains. The newest brown stems harden in the fall and take on
a rich reddish color. The red stems have 1d6+2 doses of badger thorn per fall
season. The bush retains its red thorns and thus an unmolested bush can have as
many as 4d6+8 doses.
Special Notes: Beings under 3 feet in height may be adversely affected by this
powerful Herb and are required to make a Vitality check when used. Failure
indicates the being faints for 10-30 seconds and must make similar check during
times of stress (combat, spell-casting, etc.) while the duration holds. 
Berserker Root
Type: Root
Climate: Humid Coastal, Humid Continental, Semi-arid (forest)
Magical: No
Value: 1-3 gold
Potency: 1 year, x5 if dried
Duration: 10-40 (1d4x10) minutes 
Preparation/Usage: Chewed
Powers: Causes the being to become berserk as the skill Berserking at 100%
chance.
Description: Found under black oak trees. Grows only in places a root of this
tree is exposed. It is essentially a magical offshoot of the black oak that sepa-
rates and becomes a separate root. It is a long, gray, fibrous root with a slight
bulge in its middle portions. This middle provides 1-2 doses for each year the
root has been left to grow. The root dies in 2d6 years if not harvested.
Black Lotus

Climate: Humid Coastal, Humid Continental, Semi-Arid (wetlands, swamp)
Magical: No
Value: 10-30 gold
Potency: 2 months, x4 if dried
Duration: Immediate
Preparation/Usage: flower is ground (finely) and the lightest parts separated
out, then steeped in a tea.
Powers: type XV poison, potency F.
Description: A tiny black flower, with almost no stalk. Each flower can yield 1-
3 doses.
Blood Moss
Type: Leaf
Climate: Humid Coastal, Humid Continental (swamps, wetlands)
Magical: Yes
Value: 1-4 silvers
Potency: 1 month, x6 if dried

Preparation/Usage: dried, ground, made into tea, ingested.

during any warm season. 3d4 doses is typically found per moss bank.
Special Notes: If squeezed releases a liquid that is a mild irritant and causes a
slight rash to develop if a Disease Resistance fails.

Powers: Will stop bleeding, even heavy bleeding, and heals 1d4+1 points of

Description: The red leaves of an unusual bush that appears to be a combination
of tree and bush. The red leaves seem to appear amongst the green leaves when
the sun is brightest, usually during spring and summer months. These leaves
change back to green if there is a sharp downturn in temperature or sunshine. An
average bush will yield 1d4 doses in fall and winter and 3d6+6 doses in spring
and summer months

Deaths Head
Type: Mushroom
Climate: Humid Coastal, Humid Continental, Semi-arid (forest) 
Magical: No
Value: 2-6 silver
Potency: 2 weeks, x24 if dried
Duration: Immediate
Preparation/Usage: ingested.

light. Dragons thorn bush needs sulfurous water or sediment to survive and is
thus fairly rare. Each bush can produce from 10-30 doses and once pruned pro-
duces an additional 1d6+2 per year.

Gray Wolf Bark
Type: Bark
Climate: Humid Coastal, Humid Continental, Tropical Wet and Dry (grasslands,

Magical: Yes
Value: 3-6 gold
Potency: 3 months, x4 (if prepared)
Duration: 12 hours
Preparation/Usage: dried, ground, added to oil/cream base, rubbed on the skin.
Powers: When applied to the skin it replaces an individuals scent with the
odors of a strong magical wolf. Animals with the skill Smell will become afraid
or spooked and be unable to follow the scent unless the skill Soothe is used, or
they are under magical control. Sapient beings (5+ average mental), with the
skill Smell, who use this skill successfully will smell a large wolf.
Description: This magical herb is not a bark at all. It is a fungus that causes dis-
coloration in the bark of some trees. When fully infested, bark will curl away
from the parent tree, fall to the ground and turn a dusty gray. 3d4 doses are
found at the base of infected trees.
Special Notes: There is a small (1-5%) chance that the fungus of the bark will
still be active when the balm is applied. A Disease Resistance is required in this
case or hair will become infected with the fungus. Infected hair will turn gray
over the course of 1d4+1 days and fall out. New hair grown after the infected
hair falls out will not be further infected unless exposed again to the fungus.
When all hair is lost a penalty of -2 Appearance is applied to humanoids with
hair and -4 to furry humanoids (Grahls and Chetahls).

Silver Puff
Type: Spore ball
Climate: Humid Continental (wetlands, swamp, grassland)
Magical: No
Value: 4-8 silvers
Potency: 2 weeks, x6 if sealed
Duration: 1d4 hours
Preparation/Usage: puff ball is held outward and squeezed until it pops and
spores shoot toward opponents face, within 6 feet.
Powers: Make a Poison Resistance or sleep is induced for the duration.
Description: The silver puff balls fall off the stem, they are water tight and are
usually carried away by water until the ball decays enough to release the spores.
The spores are very fine and tend to cling to clothing and the ground. Each ball
is one dose. A silver puff plant produces 1d4+2 per summer season. 
Special Notes: Requires a to hit as a missile weapon (+4 modifier). Opponents
aware of the effect of the silver puff may make a Will Force save to hold their
breath until the spores settle. Some barbaric cultures use paste or glue to stick
these spore balls on the end of crude staves, then using them to attack the head
and shoulders of opponents until the silver puffs burst.

Preparation/Usage: ground and pulped, rubbed on the skin, or dried, ground
and made into tea.
Powers: Temporarily relieves depressive insanity symptoms as well as fear both
irrational (insanity-based phobias) as well as those caused by magic or divine
magic. While under the influence of this plant the being is immune to fear. Also
heals 1-3 points if applied directly to wounds. 
Description: St. Johns Wort is a bush flowering in spring time with numerous
yellow flowers. When these flowers are pulped they produce blood red oil. Each
bush produces 2d6 doses per year.
Treasure Berries
Type: Berry
Climate: Humid Coastal, Humid Continental, Semi-arid (forest, plains, scrub-
land, grassland)
Magical: Yes
Value: 1-4 silver
Potency: 3 days, x10 if dried
Duration: immediate
Preparation/Usage: Ingested.
Powers: Each dose (3 berries) is a full days meal for an average humanoid. 
Description: Rumor has it Wood Elven Druids created treasure berries to supply
Elven hunters with food in the wild. These hearty bushes bloom year round,
whenever there is a source of water. They are large bushes that give off a dis-
tinctive odor that is unpleasant to animals. Thus they are largely unmolested
until humanoids feed off them. A typical berry laden bush has about 4d6 doses
or meals.
Special Notes: Elven custom has that for every three berries picked a fourth is
picked and then dropped a day later, thus spreading the Treasure Berries.
Tree Horn
Type: Stalk
Climate: Any that contain trees.
Magical: Yes
Value: 3-4 coppers
Potency: 6 Months, x4 if dried
Duration: 1d4+2 hours
Preparation/Usage: chewed but not ingested.
Powers: grants the ability to sense the nearest location of surface water if any is
within 10 miles. 
Description: A parasite with no leaves that grows on trees, sending a taproot
into the tree. The horns never grow very large and in their 7th year they flake
away releasing spores that float onto other trees. A Tree Horn can only grow in
cracks and wounds on the tree. Each Tree Horn is a single dose.
Special Notes: If the Tree Horn is ingested, the user is deluded and misdirected
as to the location of water.
Ubortha
Type: Fruit
Climate: Humid Continental (grasslands, forest)
Magical: Yes
Value: 10-40 platinum
Potency: 2 weeks, x2 if dried
Duration: 10-40 minutes
Preparation/Usage: ingested
Powers: Grants either a vision (similar to the skill Forsee) or allows Scrying (as
the spell) on any reflective surface. The user must concentrate on one or the
other effect and may not choose both.
Description: A twisted plum-like tree that has black plum sized fruit in fall.
Each plum is a single dose. A tree produces only 2d4+2 such fruit a season.
Special Notes: Two levels of mental fatigue are assessed after use of this herb.
Warrior Rose
Type: Flower
Climate: Humid Coastal, Semi-arid (grasslands, forest) 
Magical: Yes
Value: 4-8 platinum
Potency: 2 months, x6 if dried
Duration: 1d4 hours
Preparation/Usage: flower is chewed thoroughly and then swallowed.
Powers: Temporarily increases the physique, adding 1-3 points to each physical
attribute, and 2-5 to Endurance. In addition the being is now immune to sleep,
hold and paralysis regardless of the source.
Description: A red rose with dark blue tips, growing out of an extremely thorny
although small rose bush. 
Special Notes: Two levels of fatigue apply immediately following the duration
as well as -1 to all physical attributes for 12 hours.
White Willow Bark 
Type: Bark
Climate: Humid Coastal, Humid Continental, Semi-arid (forest) 
Magical: No
Value: 2-5 silvers
Potency: 3 months, x8 if dried
Duration: 2d4 hours
Preparation/Usage: dried, ground and ingested or made into a tea.
Powers: reduces pain, swelling and provides headache relief. One level of men-
tal fatigue is reduced. Damage is reduced by 1 point per wound. If feverish, a

Vitality save allows the fever to subside (in diseases that cause physical weak-
ness, reduce that attribute loss symptom by 1 point).
Description: The bark can be removed only in spring time when sap is flowing.
8d4 doses can be removed per tree each year without adversely effecting the
tree.
Special Notes: Modern aspirin originated from white willow bark.
White Wood (Bark)
Type: Bark
Climate: Humid Coastal, Humid Continental, Semi-Arid (ancient forest)
Magical: Yes
Value: 40-80 platinum
Potency: indefinite
Duration: Permanent
Preparation/Usage: holed with a magical awl and woven into scale armor.
Powers: Can be used to make 25 point  +1 or +2 magical (depending on the age
of the tree) wooden scale armor.  
Description: The tree sloughs off enough bark in two years time to make one
suit for an average sized humanoid.
White Wood (Sap)
Type: Sap
Climate: Humid Coastal, Humid Continental, Semi-Arid (ancient forest)
Magical: Yes
Value: 1-4 platinum (x10 for poison)
Potency: indefinite
Duration: Immediate
Preparation/Usage: ingested.
Powers: Above the ground White Wood Sap makes an excellent magical base
for mixing potions and/or other herbs that require a magical base. Sap found in
the roots is a Type XII, Potency Q magical poison. 
Description: The tree can provide 3d4 doses of sap per month without being
harmed.
White Wood (Leaves) 
Type: Leaf
Climate: Humid Coastal, Humid Continental, Semi-Arid (ancient forest)
Magical: Yes
Value: 3-8 platinum
Potency: indefinite
Duration: Immediate
Preparation/Usage: dried, ground, added to oil/cream base, and applied to the
wound or burn.
Powers: Strong magical healing (2d4).
Description: Only the fallen leaves can be used. The tree can provide 4d4 doses
of leaves per month in fall months.
White Wood (Wood)
Type: Trunk, Branches
Climate: Humid Coastal, Humid Continental, Semi-Arid (ancient forest)
Magical: Yes
Value: 20 platinum per foot
Potency: indefinite
Duration: Permanent
Preparation/Usage: carved to make weapon or part of weapon.
Powers: is a source of +1 or +2 magical wood, depending on the age of the
white wood tree.
Description: Even dead limbs can be used for this purpose.
Wizards Cap
Type: Mushroom
Climate: Humid Coastal, Humid Continental, Semi-Arid (ancient forest)
Magical: No
Value: 100-300 platinum
Potency: 2 weeks, x12 (if dried)
Duration: 1d4 hours.
Preparation/Usage: ingested.
Powers: increases spell casting ability greatly, adding 1d4+1 Aura rating, +4
Aura control and doubling Aura regeneration rate.
Description: A tapered mushroom ending in a droopy pointed cone, likened to
the wizards hat and certainly a wizards friend. Each mushroom provides a sin-
gle dose. These mushrooms grow only in the fall, usually among the fall of dead
leaves from magical tress.
Special Notes: Two levels of mental fatigue are applied immediately following
use.
Wolfs Ear
Type: Leaf
Climate: Humid Coastal, Humid Continental, Semi-Arid (forest, grassland,
plains)
Magical: Yes
Value: 9-14 gold
Potency: 2 weeks, x24 if dried
Duration: 1 day
Preparation/Usage: the hair of the leaf is scraped off and ground, then steeped
into a tea.
Powers: Suppresses lycantheropy for the duration.
Description: these reddish leaves are seen on a normally green leafed shrub in
summer months. The plant produces 3d4 of these leaves per season.
Special Notes: Lycantheropes in either changed forms (animal or half-form)
must make a Control Resistance to retain the changed form if this herb is ingest-
ed.
Zazor (Fairy Fingers)
Type: Parasitic Leaf
Climate: Forests
Magical: yes
Value: 6-12 Gold
Potency: 2 Months, x2 if dried
Duration: 6 Hours
Preparation/Usage: the leaf is chewed and swallowed. It can be dried for longer
life.
Powers: Grants +20% to Poison Resistance and +25% to Disease Resistance for
the duration. 
Description: A small, paddle-shaped, multicolor leaf that attaches itself to other
trees in the bark creases and sends a tap root into the tree stealing precious fluids
from the tree. New leaves bud off in the early spring. During the fall these new
leaves detach and fly in a circular, spinning fashion, magically seeking a healthy
tree to attach to. 
Special Notes: Also grants new Poison Resistance if affected by a poison or a
disease within the last 10 minutes.

Other Products Available For The 
Imagine Role-Playing System
The Imagine Players Guide is the culmination of
years of work and development.
Never before has anyone combined fun, creativity,
and playability into a role-playing game of this 
caliber.
Imagine a world limited only by the bounds of your
mind.
Imagine a game unlike any you have ever played
before.
Imagine no more; we have made it a reality.
Now, if you dare, discover what role-playing is all
about.
The Imagine Masters Manual is the realization of
a great undertaking.
Mammoth in scope, size, and sheer information, it
is  the perfect compliment to the Imagine Players
Guide.
Inside this tome Game Masters and players alike
will find all the keys they need to breathe life into
what is a new dimension of gaming.
Create and destroy new worlds, monsters, classes,
races, and anything else you can think of.
So dont just sit there...
Unlock the full potential of your game.
These and other Imagine products are available for purchase at
www.role-playing.com
or at fine gaming outlets worldwide.
